Mei-Lin's forehead bulges to convex dome with brows furrowed into single dense shelf, pressing leftward with her jade rings catching the registration shift; Yuna's thighs remain shortened longitudinally to one-quarter ordinary, her compressed legs splayed beneath the peplum as her copper wire frame receives pressure from both flanks; Hana's forearms are widened transversely to three times ordinary, reaching rightward with her severe up-tilted eyes and tiny pursed button mouth clamped as if sealing the border. The enormous hollow compass rose stands vertical and central, occupying forty-five percent of the canvas, its faded cardinal points now angled to read northeast-southwest while the women redistribute across its rotated face. Two identical brass declaration seals appear: one clipped to Mei-Lin's left jade ring at the northwest rim where her convex dome calculates retrieval, one pinned beneath Hana's widened right forearm at the southeast rim as her pursed mouth claims possession, the second seal casting narrow contact shadow against Yuna's compressed center where both seals remain unreachable. Mei-Lin's bulged forehead transmits lateral pressure through the compass face toward Yuna's tilted wire; Yuna's shortened thighs return downward compression into the empty socket; Hana's spread forearms redirect inward claiming force toward the center. The shallow poster plane arrays left-to-right overlap without recession, negative space pooling behind Yuna's copper frame. Yoko, 37, woman, Japanese, centered between the other two, wears a violet liquid-lamascape tunic with heat-pleated peplum and chartreuse raw-silk wide-leg boots; her gravity-defying shimada with rice-paper extensions crowns an elongated neck stretched longitudinally to three times ordinary scale, arching over the center gap of the cabinet so her chin hovers uselessly above the exposed rails she cannot bend to reach. Mei-Feng, 29, woman, Hong Kong Chinese, wears an emerald hand-woven ramie jacket with exposed selvedge and hot-pink rubberized platform clogs; her architectural undercut holds silver leaf as her cheeks are puffed to spherical fullness and her eyes squeezed to merry crescents, pressed sideways into the right cavity with her face reflecting the empty rails in mock absorption. The enormous hollow filing cabinet occupies forty percent of the canvas, its industrial carcass supporting the women within three vertical cavities while separating their poses into left, center, right arrangement on a single shallow poster plane. Two identical brass index tabs serve as paired motif: one clipped to Min-Ji's widened left forearm as her category of control, the other fallen through the exposed center rails beneath Yoko's hovering chin where her elongated neck cannot descend to retrieve it. The left-to-right A-B-C spatial order has Yoko physically centered with Min-Ji gripping the left cavity facing front and Mei-Feng pressed into the right cavity facing front, no limb reaching across another person, no body bisected by structure. Near the end, the same three East Asian adult women compose the complete cast with no additional figures.
